```
    92→// OSSProvider 定义文件上传能力。
    93→type OSSProvider interface {
    94→	// Upload 上传内容并返回可访问的 URL。
    95→	Upload(ctx context.Context, objectKey string, content []byte, contentType string) (string, error)
    96→	// Delete 删除对象。
    97→	Delete(ctx context.Context, objectKey string) error
    98→	Mock() bool
    99→}
   100→
   101→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   102→// 集合
   103→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   104→
   105→// Providers 持有全部第三方服务实现。
   106→type Providers struct {
   107→	WeChat WeChatProvider
   108→	SMS    SMSProvider
   109→	OSS    OSSProvider
   110→}
   111→
   112→// NewProviders 根据配置构建第三方服务实现集合。
   113→func NewProviders(cfg *config.Config) *Providers {
   114→	return &Providers{
   115→		WeChat: newWeChatProvider(cfg),
   116→		SMS:    newSMSProvider(cfg),
   117→		OSS:    newOSSProvider(cfg),
   118→	}
   119→}
   120→
   121→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   122→// 工具
   123→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   124→
   125→// RandomCode 生成指定长度的数字验证码。
   126→func RandomCode(length int) string {
   127→	if length <= 0 {
   128→		length = 6
   129→	}
   130→	max := big.NewInt(10)
   131→	sb := strings.Builder{}
   132→	for i := 0; i < length; i++ {
   133→		n, err := rand.Int(rand.Reader, max)
   134→		if err != nil {
   135→			sb.WriteByte('0')
   136→			continue
   137→		}
   138→		sb.WriteString(strconv.Itoa(int(n.Int64())))
   139→	}
   140→	return sb.String()
   141→}
   142→
   143→// RandomString 生成指定长度的随机字符串。
   144→func RandomString(length int) string {
   145→	if length <= 0 {
   146→		length = 16
   147→	}
   148→	buf := make([]byte, length)
   149→	_, _ = rand.Read(buf)
   150→	return hex.EncodeToString(buf)[:length]
   151→}
   152→
   153→// Sign 生成 md5 签名。
   154→func Sign(values ...string) string {
   155→	joined := strings.Join(values, "&")
   156→	sum := md5.Sum([]byte(joined))
   157→	return hex.EncodeToString(sum[:])
   158→}
   159→
   160→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   161→// 验证码存储（内存实现，生产环境可替换为 Redis）
   162→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   163→
   164→type codeStore struct {
   165→	mu   sync.RWMutex
   166→	data map[string]codeEntry
   167→}
   168→
   169→type codeEntry struct {
   170→	code      string
   171→	expiresAt time.Time
   172→	attempts  int
   173→}
   174→
   175→func newCodeStore() *codeStore {
   176→	return &codeStore{data: map[string]codeEntry{}}
   177→}
   178→
   179→func (s *codeStore) Save(key, code string, ttl time.Duration) {
   180→	s.mu.Lock()
   181→	defer s.mu.Unlock()
   182→	s.data[key] = codeEntry{code: code, expiresAt: time.Now().Add(ttl)}
   183→}
   184→
   185→func (s *codeStore) Verify(key, code string) (bool, string) {
   186→	s.mu.Lock()
   187→	defer s.mu.Unlock()
   188→	entry, ok := s.data[key]
   189→	if !ok {
   190→		return false, "验证码不存在或已失效"
   191→	}
   192→	if time.Now().After(entry.expiresAt) {
   193→		delete(s.data, key)
   194→		return false, "验证码已过期"
   195→	}
   196→	entry.attempts++
   197→	if entry.attempts > 5 {
   198→		delete(s.data, key)
   199→		return false, "验证码错误次数过多，请重新获取"
   200→	}
   201→	s.data[key] = entry
   202→	if entry.code != code {
   203→		return false, "验证码错误"
   204→	}
   205→	delete(s.data, key)
   206→	return true, ""
   207→}
   208→
   209→// CodeStore 是验证码存储的公共接口。
   210→type CodeStore interface {
   211→	Save(key, code string, ttl time.Duration)
   212→	Verify(key, code string) (bool, string)
   213→}
   214→
   215→// NewCodeStore 创建验证码存储。
   216→func NewCodeStore() CodeStore { return newCodeStore() }
   217→
   218→var _ = fmt.Sprintf
   219→var _ = zap.String
   220→var _ = shoplogger.L
```

```
    33→		Setup: func(ctx context.Context, app *bootstrap.App, srv *grpc.Server) error {

    36→
    37→			productv1.RegisterProductServiceServer(srv, handler)
    38→			productv1.RegisterCategoryServiceServer(srv, handler)
    39→			productv1.RegisterReviewServiceServer(srv, handler)

    44→	})
    45→	if err != nil {
    46→		logger.L().Error("商品服务启动失败", zap.Error(err))
    47→		os.Exit(1)
    48→	}
```

```
1→package inventory
     2→
     3→import (
     4→	"context"
     5→	"strconv"
     6→	"time"
     7→
     8→	"google.golang.org/protobuf/types/known/timestamppb"
     9→
    10→	commonv1 "github.com/shopstack/platform/gen/go/common/v1"
    11→	inventoryv1 "github.com/shopstack/platform/gen/go/inventory/v1"
    12→
    13→	"github.com/shopstack/platform/backend/internal/errs"
    14→	"github.com/shopstack/platform/backend/internal/mq"
    15→)
    16→
    17→// Service 承载库存域的业务逻辑。
    18→type Service struct {
    19→	repo *Repo
    20→	mq   *mq.Client
    21→}
    22→
    23→// NewService 构造库存服务。
    24→func NewService(repo *Repo, mqClient *mq.Client) *Service {
    25→	return &Service{repo: repo, mq: mqClient}
    26→}
    27→
    28→// GetStock 查询单个 SKU 库存。
    29→func (s *Service) GetStock(ctx context.Context, skuID int64) (*inventoryv1.Inventory, error) {
    30→	inv, err := s.repo.FindBySKU(ctx, uint64(skuID))
    31→	if err != nil {
    32→		return nil, err
    33→	}
    34→	return inventoryToProto(inv), nil
    35→}
    36→
    37→// BatchGetStock 批量查询库存。
    38→func (s *Service) BatchGetStock(ctx context.Context, skuIDs []int64) ([]*inventoryv1.Inventory, error) {
    39→	ids := make([]uint64, 0, len(skuIDs))
    40→	for _, id := range skuIDs {
    41→		if id > 0 {
    42→			ids = append(ids, uint64(id))
    43→		}
    44→	}
    45→	list, err := s.repo.FindBySKUs(ctx, ids)
    46→	if err != nil {
    47→		return nil, errs.Wrap(errs.CodeInternalError, "查询库存失败", err)
    48→	}
    49→	out := make([]*inventoryv1.Inventory, 0, len(list))
    50→	for i := range list {
    51→		out = append(out, inventoryToProto(&list[i]))
    52→	}
    53→	return out, nil
    54→}
    55→
    56→// ReserveStock 预占库存（下单时调用）。
    57→func (s *Service) ReserveStock(ctx context.Context, orderID int64, items []*inventoryv1.StockItem, bizType string) error {
    58→	if len(items) == 0 {
    59→		return errs.Invalid("没有需要预占的商品")
    60→	}
    61→	if bizType == "" {
    62→		bizType = "order"
    63→	}
    64→	bizID := strconv.FormatInt(orderID, 10)
    65→
    66→	for _, item := range items {
    67→		if item.GetQuantity() <= 0 {
    68→			continue
    69→		}
    70→		if _, err := s.repo.Reserve(ctx, uint64(item.GetSkuId()), int(item.GetQuantity()), bizType, bizID); err != nil {
    71→			// 任一 SKU 预占失败则回滚已预占的部分
    72→			s.rollbackReserve(ctx, orderID, items, bizType, bizID)
    73→			return err
    74→		}
    75→	}
    76→	return nil
    77→}
    78→
    79→// rollbackReserve 预占失败时释放已锁定的库存。
    80→func (s *Service) rollbackReserve(ctx context.Context, orderID int64, items []*inventoryv1.StockItem, bizType, bizID string) {
    81→	for _, item := range items {
    82→		if item.GetQuantity() <= 0 {
    83→			continue
    84→		}
    85→		_ = s.repo.Release(ctx, uint64(item.GetSkuId()), int(item.GetQuantity()),
    86→			bizType, bizID, "预占失败回滚")
    87→	}
    88→}
    89→
    90→// ReleaseStock 释放库存（订单取消时调用）。
    91→func (s *Service) ReleaseStock(ctx context.Context, orderID int64, items []*inventoryv1.StockItem, reason string) error {
    92→	bizID := strconv.FormatInt(orderID, 10)
    93→	for _, item := range items {
    94→		if item.GetQuantity() <= 0 {
    95→			continue
    96→		}
    97→		if err := s.repo.Release(ctx, uint64(item.GetSkuId()), int(item.GetQuantity()),
    98→			"order", bizID, reason); err != nil {
    99→			return err
   100→		}
   101→	}
   102→	return nil
   103→}
   104→
   105→// DeductStock 扣减库存（支付成功后调用）。
   106→func (s *Service) DeductStock(ctx context.Context, orderID int64, items []*inventoryv1.StockItem) error {
   107→	bizID := strconv.FormatInt(orderID, 10)
   108→	for _, item := range items {
   109→		if item.GetQuantity() <= 0 {
   110→			continue
   111→		}
   112→		if err := s.repo.Deduct(ctx, uint64(item.GetSkuId()), int(item.GetQuantity()), "order", bizID); err != nil {
   113→			return err
   114→		}
   115→	}
   116→	return nil
   117→}
   118→
   119→// AdjustStock 人工调整库存。
   120→func (s *Service) AdjustStock(ctx context.Context, skuID int64, target int32, safetyStock int32, warehouse, reason string) (*inventoryv1.Inventory, error) {
   121→	if target < 0 {
   122→		return nil, errs.Invalid("库存不能为负数")
   123→	}
   124→	inv, err := s.repo.Adjust(ctx, uint64(skuID), int(target), int(safetyStock), warehouse, reason)
   125→	if err != nil {
   126→		return nil, err
   127→	}
   128→	return inventoryToProto(inv), nil
   129→}
   130→
   131→// ListInventories 库存列表。
   132→func (s *Service) ListInventories(ctx context.Context, keyword string, productID int64, lowStockOnly bool, page *commonv1.PageRequest) (*inventoryv1.ListInventoriesResponse, error) {
   133→	pageNum, pageSize := normalizePage(page)
   134→	list, total, err := s.repo.ListInventories(ctx, keyword, uint64(productID), lowStockOnly, pageNum, pageSize)
   135→	if err != nil {
   136→		return nil, errs.Wrap(errs.CodeInternalError, "查询库存失败", err)
   137→	}
   138→	out := make([]*inventoryv1.Inventory, 0, len(list))
   139→	for i := range list {
   140→		out = append(out, inventoryToProto(&list[i]))
   141→	}
   142→	return &inventoryv1.ListInventoriesResponse{Inventories: out, Meta: buildMeta(pageNum, pageSize, total)}, nil
   143→}
   144→
   145→// ListLogs 库存流水。
   146→func (s *Service) ListLogs(ctx context.Context, skuID, productID int64, bizID string, changeType int32, page *commonv1.PageRequest) (*inventoryv1.ListInventoryLogsResponse, error) {
   147→	pageNum, pageSize := normalizePage(page)
   148→	list, total, err := s.repo.ListLogs(ctx, uint64(skuID), uint64(productID), bizID, changeType, pageNum, pageSize)
   149→	if err != nil {
   150→		return nil, errs.Wrap(errs.CodeInternalError, "查询库存流水失败", err)
   151→	}
   152→	out := make([]*inventoryv1.InventoryLog, 0, len(list))
   153→	for i := range list {
   154→		out = append(out, logToProto(&list[i]))
   155→	}
   156→	return &inventoryv1.ListInventoryLogsResponse{Logs: out, Meta: buildMeta(pageNum, pageSize, total)}, nil
   157→}
   158→
   159→// StockWarning 低库存预警。
   160→func (s *Service) StockWarning(ctx context.Context, threshold int32) (*inventoryv1.StockWarningResponse, error) {
   161→	list, err := s.repo.StockWarning(ctx, int(threshold))
   162→	if err != nil {
   163→		return nil, errs.Wrap(errs.CodeInternalError, "查询库存预警失败", err)
   164→	}
   165→	out := make([]*inventoryv1.Inventory, 0, len(list))
   166→	for i := range list {
   167→		out = append(out, inventoryToProto(&list[i]))
   168→	}
   169→	return &inventoryv1.StockWarningResponse{Inventories: out, Count: int32(len(out))}, nil
   170→}
   171→
   172→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   173→// 事件消费
   174→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   175→
   176→// Consumers 返回库存服务订阅的事件。
   177→func Consumers(queueName string) []mq.Consumer {
   178→	return []mq.Consumer{
   179→		{
   180→			Queue:      queueName + ".inventory",
   181→			RoutingKey: mq.EventOrderCreated,
   182→			Handler:    handleOrderCreated,
   183→		},
   184→		{
   185→			Queue:      queueName + ".inventory",
   186→			RoutingKey: mq.EventOrderPaid,
   187→			Handler:    handleOrderPaid,
   188→		},
   189→		{
   190→			Queue:      queueName + ".inventory",
   191→			RoutingKey: mq.EventOrderCancelled,
   192→			Handler:    handleOrderCancelled,
   193→		},
   194→	}
   195→}
   196→
   197→// handleOrderCreated 处理订单创建事件：预占库存。
   198→func handleOrderCreated(ctx context.Context, event *mq.Event) error {
   199→	orderID := payloadInt64(event, "order_id")
   200→	items := payloadItems(event, "items")
   201→	if orderID == 0 || len(items) == 0 {
   202→		return nil
   203→	}
   204→	protoItems := make([]*inventoryv1.StockItem, 0, len(items))
   205→	for _, it := range items {
   206→		protoItems = append(protoItems, &inventoryv1.StockItem{
   207→			SkuId: int64(it.SKUID), Quantity: int32(it.Quantity),
   208→		})
   209→	}
   210→	return globalService.ReserveStock(ctx, orderID, protoItems, "order")
   211→}
   212→
   213→// handleOrderPaid 处理支付成功事件：扣减锁定库存。
   214→func handleOrderPaid(ctx context.Context, event *mq.Event) error {
   215→	orderID := payloadInt64(event, "order_id")
   216→	items := payloadItems(event, "items")
   217→	if orderID == 0 || len(items) == 0 {
   218→		return nil
   219→	}
   220→	protoItems := make([]*inventoryv1.StockItem, 0, len(items))
   221→	for _, it := range items {
   222→		protoItems = append(protoItems, &inventoryv1.StockItem{
   223→			SkuId: int64(it.SKUID), Quantity: int32(it.Quantity),
   224→		})
   225→	}
   226→	return globalService.DeductStock(ctx, orderID, protoItems)
   227→}
   228→
   229→// handleOrderCancelled 处理订单取消事件：释放锁定库存。
   230→func handleOrderCancelled(ctx context.Context, event *mq.Event) error {
   231→	orderID := payloadInt64(event, "order_id")
   232→	items := payloadItems(event, "items")
   233→	if orderID == 0 || len(items) == 0 {
   234→		return nil
   235→	}
   236→	protoItems := make([]*inventoryv1.StockItem, 0, len(items))
   237→	for _, it := range items {
   238→		protoItems = append(protoItems, &inventoryv1.StockItem{
   239→			SkuId: int64(it.SKUID), Quantity: int32(it.Quantity),
   240→		})
   241→	}
   242→	return globalService.ReleaseStock(ctx, orderID, protoItems, "订单取消")
   243→}
   244→
   245→// globalService 供事件回调使用的服务实例。
   246→var globalService *Service
   247→
   248→// SetGlobalService 注册事件回调使用的服务实例。
   249→func SetGlobalService(s *Service) { globalService = s }
   250→
   251→// payloadItem 是事件负载中的库存明细。
   252→type payloadItem struct {
   253→	SKUID    uint64
   254→	Quantity int
   255→}
   256→
   257→func payloadInt64(event *mq.Event, key string) int64 {
   258→	if event == nil || event.Payload == nil {
   259→		return 0
   260→	}
   261→	switch v := event.Payload[key].(type) {
   262→	case float64:
   263→		return int64(v)
   264→	case int64:
   265→		return v
   266→	case int:
   267→		return int64(v)
   268→	}
   269→	return 0
   270→}
   271→
   272→func payloadItems(event *mq.Event, key string) []payloadItem {
   273→	if event == nil || event.Payload == nil {
   274→		return nil
   275→	}
   276→	raw, ok := event.Payload[key].([]any)
   277→	if !ok {
   278→		return nil
   279→	}
   280→	out := make([]payloadItem, 0, len(raw))
   281→	for _, item := range raw {
   282→		m, ok := item.(map[string]any)
   283→		if !ok {
   284→			continue
   285→		}
   286→		skuID := uint64(0)
   287→		switch v := m["sku_id"].(type) {
   288→		case float64:
   289→			skuID = uint64(v)
   290→		case int64:
   291→			skuID = uint64(v)
   292→		}
   293→		qty := 0
   294→		switch v := m["quantity"].(type) {
   295→		case float64:
   296→			qty = int(v)
   297→		case int64:
   298→			qty = int(v)
   299→		}
   300→		out = append(out, payloadItem{SKUID: skuID, Quantity: qty})
   301→	}
   302→	return out
   303→}
   304→
   305→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   306→// 转换辅助
   307→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   308→
   309→func inventoryToProto(inv *Inventory) *inventoryv1.Inventory {
   310→	if inv == nil {
   311→		return nil
   312→	}
   313→	return &inventoryv1.Inventory{
   314→		Id:             int64(inv.ID),
   315→		SkuId:          int64(inv.SKUID),
   316→		ProductId:      int64(inv.ProductID),
   317→		SkuCode:        inv.SKUCode,
   318→		SpecText:       inv.SpecText,
   319→		TotalStock:     int32(inv.TotalStock),
   320→		AvailableStock: int32(inv.AvailableStock),
   321→		LockedStock:    int32(inv.LockedStock),
   322→		SoldStock:      int32(inv.SoldStock),
   323→		SafetyStock:    int32(inv.SafetyStock),
   324→		Warehouse:      inv.Warehouse,
   325→		UpdatedAt:      timestamppb.New(inv.UpdatedAt),
   326→	}
   327→}
   328→
   329→func logToProto(l *InventoryLog) *inventoryv1.InventoryLog {
   330→	if l == nil {
   331→		return nil
   332→	}
   333→	return &inventoryv1.InventoryLog{
   334→		Id:          int64(l.ID),
   335→		SkuId:       int64(l.SKUID),
   336→		ProductId:   int64(l.ProductID),
   337→		SkuCode:     l.SKUCode,
   338→		ChangeType:  int32(l.ChangeType),
   339→		Quantity:    int32(l.Quantity),
   340→		BeforeStock: int32(l.BeforeStock),
   341→		AfterStock:  int32(l.AfterStock),
   342→		BizType:     l.BizType,
   343→		BizId:       l.BizID,
   344→		Operator:    l.Operator,
   345→		Remark:      l.Remark,
   346→		CreatedAt:   timestamppb.New(l.CreatedAt),
   347→	}
   348→}
   349→
   350→func normalizePage(page *commonv1.PageRequest) (int, int) {
   351→	pageNum := int(page.GetPage())
   352→	pageSize := int(page.GetPageSize())
   353→	if pageNum <= 0 {
   354→		pageNum = 1
   355→	}
   356→	if pageSize <= 0 {
   357→		pageSize = 20
   358→	}
   359→	if pageSize > 100 {
   360→		pageSize = 100
   361→	}
   362→	return pageNum, pageSize
   363→}
   364→
   365→func buildMeta(page, pageSize int, total int64) *commonv1.PageMeta {
   366→	totalPages := int64(0)
   367→	if pageSize > 0 {
   368→		totalPages = (total + int64(pageSize) - 1) / int64(pageSize)
   369→	}
   370→	return &commonv1.PageMeta{
   371→		Page: int32(page), PageSize: int32(pageSize),
   372→		Total: total, TotalPages: int32(totalPages),
   373→	}
   374→}
   375→
   376→var _ = time.Now
```

```
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS `coupons` (
  `id`                   BIGINT UNSIGNED NOT NULL AUTO_INCREMENT,
  `name`                 VARCHAR(128) NOT NULL,
  `code`                 VARCHAR(64) NOT NULL,
  `type`                 TINYINT NOT NULL DEFAULT 1 COMMENT '1满减 2折扣 3免运费',
  `face_value_cents`     BIGINT NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
  `percent`              INT NOT NULL DEFAULT 100,
  `min_amount_cents`     BIGINT NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
  `max_discount_cents`   BIGINT NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
  `total_quantity`       BIGINT NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
  `received_quantity`    BIGINT NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
  `used_quantity`        BIGINT NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
  `per_user_limit`       BIGINT NOT NULL DEFAULT 1,
  `start_time`           DATETIME NOT NULL,
  `end_time`             DATETIME NOT NULL,
  `category_id`          BIGINT UNSIGNED NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
  `enabled`              TINYINT NOT NULL DEFAULT 1,
  `created_at`           DATETIME NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
  `updated_at`           DATETIME NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP ON UPDATE CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
  PRIMARY KEY (`id`),
  UNIQUE KEY `uk_code` (`code`),
  KEY `idx_enabled_time` (`enabled`, `start_time`, `end_time`)
) ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8mb4 COMMENT='优惠券';
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS `user_coupons` (
  `id`          BIGINT UNSIGNED NOT NULL AUTO_INCREMENT,
  `user_id`     BIGINT UNSIGNED NOT NULL,
  `coupon_id`   BIGINT UNSIGNED NOT NULL,
  `status`      TINYINT NOT NULL DEFAULT 1 COMMENT '1未使用 2已使用 3已过期',
  `order_id`    BIGINT UNSIGNED NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
  `received_at` DATETIME NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
  `used_at`     DATETIME NULL,
  PRIMARY KEY (`id`),
  UNIQUE KEY `uk_user_coupon` (`user_id`, `coupon_id`),
  KEY `idx_user_status` (`user_id`, `status`)
) ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8mb4 COMMENT='用户优惠券';
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS `promotions` (
  `id`               BIGINT UNSIGNED NOT NULL AUTO_INCREMENT,
  `name`             VARCHAR(128) NOT NULL,
  `description`      VARCHAR(512) NOT NULL DEFAULT '',
  `type`             TINYINT NOT NULL DEFAULT 1 COMMENT '1满减 2折扣 3秒杀 4拼团',
  `status`           TINYINT NOT NULL DEFAULT 0 COMMENT '0未开始 1进行中 2已结束 3已下线',
  `start_time`       DATETIME NOT NULL,
  `end_time`         DATETIME NOT NULL,
  `min_amount_cents` BIGINT NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
  `discount_cents`   BIGINT NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
  `percent`          INT NOT NULL DEFAULT 100,
  `product_id`       BIGINT UNSIGNED NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
  `limit_per_user`   INT NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
  `sold_count`       BIGINT NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
  `banner_image`     VARCHAR(512) NOT NULL DEFAULT '',
  `created_at`       DATETIME NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
  `updated_at`       DATETIME NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP ON UPDATE CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
  PRIMARY KEY (`id`),
  KEY `idx_status_time` (`status`, `start_time`, `end_time`)
) ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8mb4 COMMENT='促销活动';
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS `shipments` (
  `id`             BIGINT UNSIGNED NOT NULL AUTO_INCREMENT,
  `order_id`       BIGINT UNSIGNED NOT NULL,
  `order_no`       VARCHAR(32) NOT NULL DEFAULT '',
  `tracking_no`    VARCHAR(64) NOT NULL DEFAULT '',
  `company`        VARCHAR(64) NOT NULL DEFAULT '',
  `company_code`   VARCHAR(32) NOT NULL DEFAULT '',
  `status`         TINYINT NOT NULL DEFAULT 1 COMMENT '1待发货 2已发货 3已签收 4异常',
  `receiver`       VARCHAR(64) NOT NULL DEFAULT '',
  `phone`          VARCHAR(32) NOT NULL DEFAULT '',
  `address`        VARCHAR(512) NOT NULL DEFAULT '',
  `freight_cents`  BIGINT NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
  `item_count`     INT NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
  `remark`         VARCHAR(512) NOT NULL DEFAULT '',
  `shipped_at`     DATETIME NULL,
  `delivered_at`   DATETIME NULL,
  `created_at`     DATETIME NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
  `updated_at`     DATETIME NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP ON UPDATE CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
  PRIMARY KEY (`id`),
  KEY `idx_order` (`order_id`),
  KEY `idx_tracking` (`tracking_no`),
  KEY `idx_status` (`status`)
) ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8mb4 COMMENT='发货单';
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS `shipment_tracks` (
  `id`           BIGINT UNSIGNED NOT NULL AUTO_INCREMENT,
  `shipment_id`  BIGINT UNSIGNED NOT NULL,
  `tracking_no`  VARCHAR(64) NOT NULL DEFAULT '',
  `location`     VARCHAR(128) NOT NULL DEFAULT '',
  `description`  VARCHAR(512) NOT NULL DEFAULT '',
  `occurred_at`  DATETIME NOT NULL,
  `sort`         INT NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
  PRIMARY KEY (`id`),
  KEY `idx_shipment` (`shipment_id`, `sort`)
) ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8mb4 COMMENT='物流轨迹';
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS `logistic_templates` (
  `id`                        BIGINT UNSIGNED NOT NULL AUTO_INCREMENT,
  `name`                      VARCHAR(64) NOT NULL,
  `company`                   VARCHAR(64) NOT NULL DEFAULT '',
  `company_code`              VARCHAR(32) NOT NULL DEFAULT '',
  `freight_cents`             BIGINT NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
  `free_freight_amount_cents` BIGINT NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
  `regions`                   VARCHAR(512) NOT NULL DEFAULT '',
  `enabled`                   TINYINT NOT NULL DEFAULT 1,
  `sort`                      INT NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
  `created_at`                DATETIME NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
  `updated_at`                DATETIME NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP ON UPDATE CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
  PRIMARY KEY (`id`)
) ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8mb4 COMMENT='运费模板';
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS `notifications` (
  `id`         BIGINT UNSIGNED NOT NULL AUTO_INCREMENT,
  `user_id`    BIGINT UNSIGNED NOT NULL,
  `type`       TINYINT NOT NULL DEFAULT 1 COMMENT '1订单 2物流 3营销 4系统',
  `title`      VARCHAR(200) NOT NULL DEFAULT '',
  `content`    VARCHAR(1024) NOT NULL DEFAULT '',
  `link`       VARCHAR(512) NOT NULL DEFAULT '',
  `biz_id`     BIGINT UNSIGNED NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
  `read`       TINYINT NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
  `read_at`    DATETIME NULL,
  `created_at` DATETIME NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
  PRIMARY KEY (`id`),
  KEY `idx_user_read` (`user_id`, `read`),
  KEY `idx_created` (`created_at`)
) ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8mb4 COMMENT='站内通知';
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS `sms_records` (
  `id`            BIGINT UNSIGNED NOT NULL AUTO_INCREMENT,
  `phone`         VARCHAR(32) NOT NULL,
  `code`          VARCHAR(16) NOT NULL DEFAULT '',
  `scene`         VARCHAR(32) NOT NULL DEFAULT '',
  `status`        TINYINT NOT NULL DEFAULT 1 COMMENT '1成功 0失败',
  `error_message` VARCHAR(512) NOT NULL DEFAULT '',
  `send_count`    INT NOT NULL DEFAULT 1,
  `created_at`    DATETIME NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
  PRIMARY KEY (`id`),
  KEY `idx_phone` (`phone`),
  KEY `idx_created` (`created_at`)
) ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8mb4 COMMENT='短信发送记录';
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS `audit_logs` (
  `id`            BIGINT UNSIGNED NOT NULL AUTO_INCREMENT,
  `admin_id`      BIGINT UNSIGNED NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
  `admin_name`    VARCHAR(64) NOT NULL DEFAULT '',
  `module`        VARCHAR(64) NOT NULL DEFAULT '',
  `action`        VARCHAR(64) NOT NULL DEFAULT '',
  `target_type`   VARCHAR(64) NOT NULL DEFAULT '',
  `target_id`     VARCHAR(64) NOT NULL DEFAULT '',
  `detail`        TEXT,
  `ip`            VARCHAR(64) NOT NULL DEFAULT '',
  `user_agent`    VARCHAR(512) NOT NULL DEFAULT '',
  `result`        TINYINT NOT NULL DEFAULT 1,
  `error_message` VARCHAR(512) NOT NULL DEFAULT '',
  `created_at`    DATETIME NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
  PRIMARY KEY (`id`),
  KEY `idx_module_action` (`module`, `action`),
  KEY `idx_admin` (`admin_id`),
  KEY `idx_created` (`created_at`)
) ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8mb4 COMMENT='操作审计日志';
```

```
1→// Package cache 封装 Redis 客户端，提供缓存与分布式锁能力。
     2→package cache
     3→
     4→import (
     5→	"context"
     6→	"encoding/json"
     7→	"fmt"
     8→	"time"
     9→
    10→	"github.com/redis/go-redis/v9"
    11→	"go.uber.org/zap"
    12→
    13→	shoplogger "github.com/shopstack/platform/backend/internal/logger"
    14→)
    15→
    16→// Client 包装 redis.Client。
    17→type Client struct {
    18→	rdb *redis.Client
    19→}
    20→
    21→// Options 控制 Redis 连接行为。
    22→type Options struct {
    23→	Addr     string
    24→	Password string
    25→	DB       int
    26→	PoolSize int
    27→}
    28→
    29→// New 创建并连通 Redis 客户端。
    30→func New(opts Options) (*Client, error) {
    31→	if opts.PoolSize <= 0 {
    32→		opts.PoolSize = 50
    33→	}
    34→	rdb := redis.NewClient(&redis.Options{
    35→		Addr:         opts.Addr,
    36→		Password:     opts.Password,
    37→		DB:           opts.DB,
    38→		PoolSize:     opts.PoolSize,
    39→		DialTimeout:  5 * time.Second,
    40→		ReadTimeout:  3 * time.Second,
    41→		WriteTimeout: 3 * time.Second,
    42→	})
    43→	ctx, cancel := context.WithTimeout(context.Background(), 5*time.Second)
    44→	defer cancel()
    45→	if err := rdb.Ping(ctx).Err(); err != nil {
    46→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("Redis 连通性检查失败: %w", err)
    47→	}
    48→	return &Client{rdb: rdb}, nil
    49→}
    50→
    51→// Client 暴露底层 redis 客户端。
    52→func (c *Client) Client() *redis.Client { return c.rdb }
    53→
    54→// Close 关闭连接。
    55→func (c *Client) Close() error { return c.rdb.Close() }
    56→
    57→// GetString 读取字符串。
    58→func (c *Client) GetString(ctx context.Context, key string) (string, error) {
    59→	return c.rdb.Get(ctx, key).Result()
    60→}
    61→
    62→// SetString 写入字符串。
    63→func (c *Client) SetString(ctx context.Context, key, value string, ttl time.Duration) error {
    64→	return c.rdb.Set(ctx, key, value, ttl).Err()
    65→}
    66→
    67→// Del 删除键。
    68→func (c *Client) Del(ctx context.Context, keys ...string) error {
    69→	if len(keys) == 0 {
    70→		return nil
    71→	}
    72→	return c.rdb.Del(ctx, keys...).Err()
    73→}
    74→
    75→// Exists 判断键是否存在。
    76→func (c *Client) Exists(ctx context.Context, key string) (bool, error) {
    77→	n, err := c.rdb.Exists(ctx, key).Result()
    78→	return n > 0, err
    79→}
    80→
    81→// Incr 自增并返回新值。
    82→func (c *Client) Incr(ctx context.Context, key string) (int64, error) {
    83→	return c.rdb.Incr(ctx, key).Result()
    84→}
    85→
    86→// Decr 自减。
    87→func (c *Client) Decr(ctx context.Context, key string) (int64, error) {
    88→	return c.rdb.Decr(ctx, key).Result()
    89→}
    90→
    91→// Expire 设置过期时间。
    92→func (c *Client) Expire(ctx context.Context, key string, ttl time.Duration) error {
    93→	return c.rdb.Expire(ctx, key, ttl).Err()
    94→}
    95→
    96→// GetJSON 读取并反序列化 JSON。
    97→func (c *Client) GetJSON(ctx context.Context, key string, dest any) error {
    98→	data, err := c.rdb.Get(ctx, key).Bytes()
    99→	if err != nil {
   100→		return err
   101→	}
   102→	return json.Unmarshal(data, dest)
   103→}
   104→
   105→// SetJSON 序列化并写入 JSON。
   106→func (c *Client) SetJSON(ctx context.Context, key string, value any, ttl time.Duration) error {
   107→	data, err := json.Marshal(value)
   108→	if err != nil {
   109→		return err
   110→	}
   111→	return c.rdb.Set(ctx, key, data, ttl).Err()
   112→}
   113→
   114→// SetNX 分布式锁：仅当 key 不存在时设置成功。
   115→func (c *Client) SetNX(ctx context.Context, key, value string, ttl time.Duration) (bool, error) {
   116→	return c.rdb.SetNX(ctx, key, value, ttl).Result()
   117→}
   118→
   119→// DelByPattern 按模式删除键。
   120→func (c *Client) DelByPattern(ctx context.Context, pattern string) error {
   121→	iter := c.rdb.Scan(ctx, 0, pattern, 100).Iterator()
   122→	var keys []string
   123→	for iter.Next(ctx) {
   124→		keys = append(keys, iter.Val())
   125→	}
   126→	if err := iter.Err(); err != nil {
   127→		return err
   128→	}
   129→	if len(keys) > 0 {
   130→		return c.rdb.Del(ctx, keys...).Err()
   131→	}
   132→	return nil
   133→}
   134→
   135→// Lock 是一个简易的 Redis 分布式锁。
   136→type Lock struct {
   137→	client *Client
   138→	key    string
   139→	value  string
   140→	ttl    time.Duration
   141→}
   142→
   143→// AcquireLock 尝试获取分布式锁。
   144→func (c *Client) AcquireLock(ctx context.Context, key string, ttl time.Duration) (*Lock, bool, error) {
   145→	value := fmt.Sprintf("%d", time.Now().UnixNano())
   146→	ok, err := c.SetNX(ctx, key, value, ttl)
   147→	if err != nil {
   148→		return nil, false, err
   149→	}
   150→	if !ok {
   151→		return nil, false, nil
   152→	}
   153→	return &Lock{client: c, key: key, value: value, ttl: ttl}, true, nil
   154→}
   155→
   156→// Release 释放锁（仅释放自己持有的锁）。
   157→func (l *Lock) Release(ctx context.Context) error {
   158→	const script = `
   159→	if redis.call("get", KEYS[1]) == ARGV[1] then
   160→		return redis.call("del", KEYS[1])
   161→	else
   162→		return 0
   163→	end`
   164→	return l.client.rdb.Eval(ctx, script, []string{l.key}, l.value).Err()
   165→}
   166→
   167→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   168→// 业务缓存键
   169→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   170→
   171→const (
   172→	KeyProductPrefix   = "shop:product:"
   173→	KeyCategoryPrefix  = "shop:category:"
   174→	KeyCartPrefix      = "shop:cart:"
   175→	KeyUserPrefix      = "shop:user:"
   176→	KeyStockPrefix     = "shop:stock:"
   177→	KeyHomeBanner      = "shop:home:banner"
   178→	KeyHomeCategories  = "shop:home:categories"
   179→	KeyHomeRecommend   = "shop:home:recommend"
   180→	KeySearchPrefix    = "shop:search:"
   181→	KeyAdminPermission = "shop:admin:perm:"
   182→)
   183→
   184→// CacheKey 按前缀与 ID 拼接缓存键。
   185→func CacheKey(prefix string, id int64) string {
   186→	return fmt.Sprintf("%s%d", prefix, id)
   187→}
   188→
   189→// LogCacheError 记录缓存错误但不中断业务流程。
   190→func LogCacheError(op string, err error) {
   191→	if err == nil || err == redis.Nil {
   192→		return
   193→	}
   194→	shoplogger.L().Warn("Redis 操作失败", zap.String("操作", op), zap.Error(err))
   195→}
```

</details>

```
1→// Package config 负责加载和解析微服务配置。
     2→//
     3→// 配置优先级（从低到高）：
     4→//  1. 内置默认值
     5→//  2. YAML 配置文件
     6→//  3. 环境变量（前缀 SHOP_，如 SHOP_SERVER_GRPC_ADDR）
     7→package config
     8→
     9→import (
    10→	"fmt"
    11→	"os"
    12→	"path/filepath"
    13→	"strconv"
    14→	"strings"
    15→	"sync"
    16→	"time"
    17→
    18→	"gopkg.in/yaml.v3"
    19→)
    20→
    21→type Config struct {
    22→	ServiceName string            `yaml:"service_name"`
    23→	Env         string            `yaml:"env"`
    24→	Server      ServerConfig      `yaml:"server"`
    25→	MySQL       MySQLConfig       `yaml:"mysql"`
    26→	Redis       RedisConfig       `yaml:"redis"`
    27→	RabbitMQ    RabbitMQConfig    `yaml:"rabbitmq"`
    28→	Nacos       NacosConfig       `yaml:"nacos"`
    29→	JWT         JWTConfig         `yaml:"jwt"`
    30→	Log         LogConfig         `yaml:"log"`
    31→	ThirdParty  ThirdPartyConfig  `yaml:"third_party"`
    32→	Services    map[string]string `yaml:"services"`
    33→}
    34→
    35→type ServerConfig struct {
    36→	GRPCAddr string `yaml:"grpc_addr"`
    37→	HTTPAddr string `yaml:"http_addr"`
    38→	Timeout  int    `yaml:"timeout"`
    39→}
    40→
    41→type MySQLConfig struct {
    42→	Host     string   `yaml:"host"`
    43→	Port     int      `yaml:"port"`
    44→	User     string   `yaml:"user"`
    45→	Password string   `yaml:"password"`
    46→	Database string   `yaml:"database"`
    47→	Charset  string   `yaml:"charset"`
    48→	MaxIdle  int      `yaml:"max_idle"`
    49→	MaxOpen  int      `yaml:"max_open"`
    50→	Extra    []string `yaml:"extra"`
    51→}
    52→
    53→func (m MySQLConfig) DSN() string {
    54→	charset := m.Charset
    55→	if charset == "" {
    56→		charset = "utf8mb4"
    57→	}
    58→	extra := strings.Join(m.Extra, "&")
    59→	if extra != "" {
    60→		extra = "&" + extra
    61→	}
    62→	return fmt.Sprintf("%s:%s@tcp(%s:%d)/%s?charset=%s&parseTime=True&loc=Local%s",
    63→		m.User, m.Password, m.Host, m.Port, m.Database, charset, extra)
    64→}
    65→
    66→type RedisConfig struct {
    67→	Addr     string `yaml:"addr"`
    68→	Password string `yaml:"password"`
    69→	DB       int    `yaml:"db"`
    70→	PoolSize int    `yaml:"pool_size"`
    71→}
    72→
    73→type RabbitMQConfig struct {
    74→	URL          string `yaml:"url"`
    75→	Exchange     string `yaml:"exchange"`
    76→	ExchangeType string `yaml:"exchange_type"`
    77→	Prefetch     int    `yaml:"prefetch"`
    78→}
    79→
    80→type NacosConfig struct {
    81→	Enabled      bool   `yaml:"enabled"`
    82→	ServerAddr   string `yaml:"server_addr"`
    83→	Namespace    string `yaml:"namespace"`
    84→	Group        string `yaml:"group"`
    85→	Username     string `yaml:"username"`
    86→	Password     string `yaml:"password"`
    87→	RegisterIP   string `yaml:"register_ip"`
    88→	RegisterPort int    `yaml:"register_port"`
    89→}
    90→
    91→type JWTConfig struct {
    92→	Secret          string `yaml:"secret"`
    93→	AccessExpire    int    `yaml:"access_expire"`     // 秒
    94→	RefreshExpire   int    `yaml:"refresh_expire"`    // 秒
    95→	AdminSecret     string `yaml:"admin_secret"`
    96→	AdminAccessExp  int    `yaml:"admin_access_expire"`
    97→	AdminRefreshExp int    `yaml:"admin_refresh_expire"`
    98→}
    99→
   100→type LogConfig struct {
   101→	Level    string `yaml:"level"`
   102→	Format   string `yaml:"format"` // json | console
   103→	Output   string `yaml:"output"`
   104→	Filename string `yaml:"filename"`
   105→}
   106→
   107→type ThirdPartyConfig struct {
   108→	WeChatAppID     string `yaml:"wechat_app_id"`
   109→	WeChatAppSecret string `yaml:"wechat_app_secret"`
   110→	WeChatMchID     string `yaml:"wechat_mch_id"`
   111→	WeChatAPIKey    string `yaml:"wechat_api_key"`
   112→	WeChatNotifyURL string `yaml:"wechat_notify_url"`
   113→	AlipayAppID     string `yaml:"alipay_app_id"`
   114→	AlipayPrivate   string `yaml:"alipay_private_key"`
   115→	AlipayPublic    string `yaml:"alipay_public_key"`
   116→	SMSProvider     string `yaml:"sms_provider"`
   117→	SMSAccessKey    string `yaml:"sms_access_key"`
   118→	SMSSecret       string `yaml:"sms_secret"`
   119→	SMSSignName     string `yaml:"sms_sign_name"`
   120→	OSSProvider     string `yaml:"oss_provider"`
   121→	OSSAccessKey    string `yaml:"oss_access_key"`
   122→	OSSSecretKey    string `yaml:"oss_secret_key"`
   123→	OSSBucket       string `yaml:"oss_bucket"`
   124→	OSSEndpoint     string `yaml:"oss_endpoint"`
   125→	OSSPublicURL    string `yaml:"oss_public_url"`
   126→}
   127→
   128→// Default 返回内置默认配置。
   129→func Default() *Config {
   130→	return &Config{
   131→		ServiceName: "unknown",
   132→		Env:         "dev",
   133→		Server:      ServerConfig{GRPCAddr: ":9000", HTTPAddr: ":8080", Timeout: 30},
   134→		MySQL: MySQLConfig{
   135→			Host: "127.0.0.1", Port: 3306, User: "shopstack", Password: "shopstack",
   136→			Database: "shopstack", Charset: "utf8mb4", MaxIdle: 10, MaxOpen: 100,
   137→			Extra: []string{"parseTime=True", "loc=Local"},
   138→		},
   139→		Redis:    RedisConfig{Addr: "127.0.0.1:6379", DB: 0, PoolSize: 50},
   140→		RabbitMQ: RabbitMQConfig{URL: "amqp://shopstack:shopstack@127.0.0.1:5672/", Exchange: "shopstack.events", ExchangeType: "topic", Prefetch: 10},
   141→		Nacos:    NacosConfig{Enabled: false, ServerAddr: "127.0.0.1:8848", Namespace: "public", Group: "DEFAULT_GROUP"},
   142→		JWT: JWTConfig{
   143→			Secret: "shopstack-dev-secret-change-me", AccessExpire: 7200, RefreshExpire: 604800,
   144→			AdminSecret: "shopstack-dev-admin-secret-change-me", AdminAccessExp: 7200, AdminRefreshExp: 604800,
   145→		},
   146→		Log:        LogConfig{Level: "info", Format: "console", Output: "stdout"},
   147→		ThirdParty: ThirdPartyConfig{SMSProvider: "mock", OSSProvider: "mock"},
   148→		Services:   map[string]string{},
   149→	}
   150→}
   151→
   152→// Load 读取 YAML 配置文件并应用环境变量覆盖。
   153→func Load(path string) (*Config, error) {
   154→	cfg := Default()
   155→
   156→	if path != "" {
   157→		data, err := os.ReadFile(path)
   158→		if err != nil {
   159→			return nil, fmt.Errorf("读取配置文件 %s 失败: %w", path, err)
   160→		}
   161→		if err := yaml.Unmarshal(data, cfg); err != nil {
   162→			return nil, fmt.Errorf("解析配置文件 %s 失败: %w", path, err)
   163→		}
   164→	}
   165→
   166→	applyEnvOverrides(cfg)
   167→	return cfg, nil
   168→}
   169→
   170→// LoadDefault 依次尝试常见位置加载配置文件。
   171→//
   172→// 找不到配置文件时回退到内置默认配置，并同样应用环境变量覆盖，
   173→// 保证仅用环境变量也能完成部署。
   174→func LoadDefault(name string) (*Config, error) {
   175→	candidates := []string{
   176→		os.Getenv("SHOP_CONFIG"),
   177→		filepath.Join("configs", name+".yaml"),
   178→		filepath.Join("..", "configs", name+".yaml"),
   179→		filepath.Join("..", "..", "configs", name+".yaml"),
   180→		filepath.Join("/", "app", "configs", name+".yaml"),
   181→	}
   182→	for _, c := range candidates {
   183→		if c == "" {
   184→			continue
   185→		}
   186→		if _, err := os.Stat(c); err == nil {
   187→			return Load(c)
   188→		}
   189→	}
   190→	cfg := Default()
   191→	applyEnvOverrides(cfg)
   192→	return cfg, nil
   193→}
   194→
   195→func applyEnvOverrides(cfg *Config) {
   196→	envStr("SHOP_SERVICE_NAME", &cfg.ServiceName)
   197→	envStr("SHOP_ENV", &cfg.Env)
   198→	envStr("SHOP_GRPC_ADDR", &cfg.Server.GRPCAddr)
   199→	envStr("SHOP_HTTP_ADDR", &cfg.Server.HTTPAddr)
   200→
   201→	envStr("SHOP_MYSQL_HOST", &cfg.MySQL.Host)
   202→	envInt("SHOP_MYSQL_PORT", &cfg.MySQL.Port)
   203→	envStr("SHOP_MYSQL_USER", &cfg.MySQL.User)
   204→	envStr("SHOP_MYSQL_PASSWORD", &cfg.MySQL.Password)
   205→	envStr("SHOP_MYSQL_DATABASE", &cfg.MySQL.Database)
   206→
   207→	envStr("SHOP_REDIS_ADDR", &cfg.Redis.Addr)
   208→	envStr("SHOP_REDIS_PASSWORD", &cfg.Redis.Password)
   209→	envInt("SHOP_REDIS_DB", &cfg.Redis.DB)
   210→
   211→	envStr("SHOP_RABBITMQ_URL", &cfg.RabbitMQ.URL)
   212→
   213→	envStr("SHOP_NACOS_ADDR", &cfg.Nacos.ServerAddr)
   214→	envBool("SHOP_NACOS_ENABLED", &cfg.Nacos.Enabled)
   215→
   216→	envStr("SHOP_JWT_SECRET", &cfg.JWT.Secret)
   217→	envStr("SHOP_ADMIN_JWT_SECRET", &cfg.JWT.AdminSecret)
   218→
   219→	envStr("SHOP_WECHAT_APP_ID", &cfg.ThirdParty.WeChatAppID)
   220→	envStr("SHOP_WECHAT_APP_SECRET", &cfg.ThirdParty.WeChatAppSecret)
   221→	envStr("SHOP_WECHAT_MCH_ID", &cfg.ThirdParty.WeChatMchID)
   222→	envStr("SHOP_WECHAT_API_KEY", &cfg.ThirdParty.WeChatAPIKey)
   223→	envStr("SHOP_SMS_ACCESS_KEY", &cfg.ThirdParty.SMSAccessKey)
   224→	envStr("SHOP_SMS_SECRET", &cfg.ThirdParty.SMSSecret)
   225→	envStr("SHOP_OSS_ACCESS_KEY", &cfg.ThirdParty.OSSAccessKey)
   226→	envStr("SHOP_OSS_SECRET_KEY", &cfg.ThirdParty.OSSSecretKey)
   227→	envStr("SHOP_OSS_BUCKET", &cfg.ThirdParty.OSSBucket)
   228→	envStr("SHOP_OSS_ENDPOINT", &cfg.ThirdParty.OSSEndpoint)
   229→}
   230→
   231→// ServiceAddr 返回指定依赖服务的 gRPC 地址。
   232→func (c *Config) ServiceAddr(name string) string {
   233→	if addr, ok := c.Services[name]; ok && addr != "" {
   234→		return addr
   235→	}
   236→	return defaultServiceAddr(name)
   237→}
   238→
   239→var defaultAddrs = map[string]string{
   240→	"user":         "127.0.0.1:9001",
   241→	"product":      "127.0.0.1:9002",
   242→	"cart":         "127.0.0.1:9003",
   243→	"order":        "127.0.0.1:9004",
   244→	"payment":      "127.0.0.1:9005",
   245→	"inventory":    "127.0.0.1:9006",
   246→	"marketing":    "127.0.0.1:9007",
   247→	"logistics":    "127.0.0.1:9008",
   248→	"notification": "127.0.0.1:9009",
   249→}
   250→
   251→func defaultServiceAddr(name string) string {
   252→	if a, ok := defaultAddrs[name]; ok {
   253→		return a
   254→	}
   255→	return ""
   256→}
   257→
   258→// AllServiceAddrs 返回所有已知服务的地址映射。
   259→func (c *Config) AllServiceAddrs() map[string]string {
   260→	out := make(map[string]string, len(defaultAddrs))
   261→	for k, v := range defaultAddrs {
   262→		out[k] = v
   263→	}
   264→	for k, v := range c.Services {
   265→		out[k] = v
   266→	}
   267→	return out
   268→}
   269→
   270→func envStr(key string, target *string) {
   271→	if v, ok := os.LookupEnv(key); ok && v != "" {
   272→		*target = v
   273→	}
   274→}
   275→
   276→func envInt(key string, target *int) {
   277→	if v, ok := os.LookupEnv(key); ok && v != "" {
   278→		if n, err := strconv.Atoi(v); err == nil {
   279→			*target = n
   280→		}
   281→	}
   282→}
   283→
   284→func envBool(key string, target *bool) {
   285→	if v, ok := os.LookupEnv(key); ok && v != "" {
   286→		if b, err := strconv.ParseBool(v); err == nil {
   287→			*target = b
   288→		}
   289→	}
   290→}
   291→
   292→var (
   293→	once sync.Once
   294→	inst *Config
   295→)
   296→
   297→// Global 返回进程级共享配置实例。
   298→func Global() *Config {
   299→	once.Do(func() {
   300→		c, err := LoadDefault(os.Getenv("SHOP_SERVICE_NAME"))
   301→		if err != nil {
   302→			c = Default()
   303→		}
   304→		inst = c
   305→	})
   306→	return inst
   307→}
   308→
   309→// AccessExpireDuration 返回访问令牌有效期。
   310→func (c *Config) AccessExpireDuration() time.Duration {
   311→	return time.Duration(c.JWT.AccessExpire) * time.Second
   312→}
```

```
1→package product
     2→
     3→import (
     4→	"context"
     5→	"encoding/json"
     6→	"strconv"
     7→	"strings"
     8→	"time"
     9→
    10→	"google.golang.org/protobuf/types/known/timestamppb"
    11→
    12→	commonv1 "github.com/shopstack/platform/gen/go/common/v1"
    13→	productv1 "github.com/shopstack/platform/gen/go/product/v1"
    14→
    15→	"github.com/shopstack/platform/backend/internal/errs"
    16→	"github.com/shopstack/platform/backend/internal/mq"
    17→)
    18→
    19→// Service 承载商品域的业务逻辑。
    20→type Service struct {
    21→	repo *Repo
    22→	mq   *mq.Client
    23→}
    24→
    25→// NewService 构造商品服务。
    26→func NewService(repo *Repo, mqClient *mq.Client) *Service {
    27→	return &Service{repo: repo, mq: mqClient}
    28→}
    29→
    30→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    31→// 商品列表与详情
    32→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    33→
    34→// ListProducts 分页查询商品。
    35→func (s *Service) ListProducts(ctx context.Context, req *productv1.ListProductsRequest) (*productv1.ListProductsResponse, error) {
    36→	page, pageSize := normalizePage(req.GetPage())
    37→
    38→	list, total, err := s.repo.ListProducts(ctx, ProductQuery{
    39→		Keyword:       strings.TrimSpace(req.GetKeyword()),
    40→		CategoryID:    uint64(req.GetCategoryId()),
    41→		Status:        int8(req.GetStatus()),
    42→		MinPriceCents: req.GetMinPriceCents(),
    43→		MaxPriceCents: req.GetMaxPriceCents(),
    44→		OnlyInStock:   req.GetOnlyInStock(),
    45→		Tags:          req.GetTags(),
    46→		Sort:          req.GetSort(),
    47→		Page:          page,
    48→		PageSize:      pageSize,
    49→	})
    50→	if err != nil {
    51→		return nil, errs.Wrap(errs.CodeInternalError, "查询商品失败", err)
    52→	}
    53→
    54→	categoryNames, err := s.categoryNameMap(ctx)
    55→	if err != nil {
    56→		categoryNames = map[uint64]string{}
    57→	}
    58→
    59→	products := make([]*productv1.Product, 0, len(list))
    60→	for i := range list {
    61→		products = append(products, s.productToProto(&list[i], categoryNames))
    62→	}
    63→
    64→	return &productv1.ListProductsResponse{
    65→		Products: products,
    66→		Meta:     buildMeta(page, pageSize, total),
    67→	}, nil
    68→}
    69→
    70→// GetProduct 查询商品详情。
    71→func (s *Service) GetProduct(ctx context.Context, req *productv1.GetProductRequest) (*productv1.ProductDetail, error) {
    72→	p, err := s.repo.FindProduct(ctx, uint64(req.GetId()))
    73→	if err != nil {
    74→		return nil, err
    75→	}
    76→
    77→	skus, err := s.repo.ProductSkus(ctx, p.ID)
    78→	if err != nil {
    79→		return nil, errs.Wrap(errs.CodeInternalError, "查询商品规格失败", err)
    80→	}
    81→	images, err := s.repo.ProductImages(ctx, p.ID)
    82→	if err != nil {
```

```
1→// Package client 提供 gRPC 客户端连接池与服务发现能力。
     2→//
     3→// 服务发现支持两种模式：
     4→//   - 静态模式（默认）：直接从配置读取目标服务地址，零外部依赖，开箱即用。
     5→//   - Nacos 模式：配置 nacos.enabled=true 后，服务实例注册到 Nacos 并动态发现。
     6→package client
     7→
     8→import (
     9→	"context"
    10→	"fmt"
    11→	"strconv"
    12→	"strings"
    13→	"sync"
    14→	"time"
    15→
    16→	"go.uber.org/zap"
    17→	"google.golang.org/grpc"
    18→	"google.golang.org/grpc/credentials/insecure"
    19→	"google.golang.org/grpc/metadata"
    20→
    21→	"github.com/shopstack/platform/backend/internal/auth"
    22→	"github.com/shopstack/platform/backend/internal/config"
    23→	shoplogger "github.com/shopstack/platform/backend/internal/logger"
    24→	"github.com/shopstack/platform/backend/internal/registry"
    25→)
    26→
    27→// Registry 管理到各微服务的 gRPC 连接。
    28→type Registry struct {
    29→	cfg   *config.Config
    30→	mu    sync.RWMutex
    31→	conns map[string]*grpc.ClientConn
    32→	nacos *registry.NacosClient
    33→}
    34→
    35→// NewRegistry 创建注册中心。Nacos 初始化失败时降级为静态模式，不阻塞启动。
    36→func NewRegistry(cfg *config.Config) *Registry {
    37→	r := &Registry{cfg: cfg, conns: map[string]*grpc.ClientConn{}}
    38→	if cfg.Nacos.Enabled {
    39→		r.nacos = registry.NewNacosClient(registry.Options{
    40→			ServerAddr: cfg.Nacos.ServerAddr,
    41→			Namespace:  cfg.Nacos.Namespace,
    42→			Group:      cfg.Nacos.Group,
    43→			Username:   cfg.Nacos.Username,
    44→			Password:   cfg.Nacos.Password,
    45→		})
    46→		shoplogger.L().Info("Nacos 服务发现已启用",
    47→			zap.String("地址", cfg.Nacos.ServerAddr),
    48→			zap.String("命名空间", cfg.Nacos.Namespace))
    49→	}
    50→	return r
    51→}
    52→
    53→// Conn 返回目标服务的 gRPC 连接（带缓存）。
    54→func (r *Registry) Conn(ctx context.Context, serviceName string) (*grpc.ClientConn, error) {
    55→	r.mu.RLock()
    56→	conn, ok := r.conns[serviceName]
    57→	r.mu.RUnlock()
    58→	if ok {
    59→		return conn, nil
    60→	}
    61→
    62→	target, err := r.resolve(ctx, serviceName)
    63→	if err != nil {
    64→		return nil, err
    65→	}
    66→
    67→	newConn, err := grpc.NewClient(target,
    68→		grpc.WithTransportCredentials(insecure.NewCredentials()),
    69→		grpc.WithChainUnaryInterceptor(identityInterceptor()),
    70→		grpc.WithChainStreamInterceptor(identityStreamInterceptor()),
    71→		grpc.WithDefaultCallOptions(
    72→			grpc.MaxCallRecvMsgSize(16*1024*1024),
    73→			grpc.MaxCallSendMsgSize(16*1024*1024),
    74→		),
    75→	)
    76→	if err != nil {
    77→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("连接服务 %s (%s) 失败: %w", serviceName, target, err)
    78→	}
    79→
    80→	r.mu.Lock()
    81→	if existing, ok := r.conns[serviceName]; ok {
    82→		r.mu.Unlock()
    83→		_ = newConn.Close()
    84→		return existing, nil
    85→	}
    86→	r.conns[serviceName] = newConn
    87→	r.mu.Unlock()
    88→
    89→	shoplogger.L().Info("已建立服务连接",
    90→		zap.String("服务", serviceName), zap.String("地址", target))
    91→	return newConn, nil
    92→}
    93→
    94→// resolve 解析服务地址。Nacos 模式下优先使用注册中心实例。
    95→func (r *Registry) resolve(ctx context.Context, serviceName string) (string, error) {
    96→	if r.nacos != nil {
    97→		instances, err := r.nacos.SelectInstances(ctx, serviceName, true)
    98→		if err == nil && len(instances) > 0 {
    99→			return instances[0].Addr(), nil
   100→		}
   101→		if err != nil {
   102→			shoplogger.L().Warn("Nacos 服务发现失败，回退静态地址",
   103→				zap.String("服务", serviceName), zap.Error(err))
   104→		}
   105→	}
   106→	addr := r.cfg.ServiceAddr(serviceName)
   107→	if addr == "" {
   108→		return "", fmt.Errorf("未找到服务 %s 的地址配置", serviceName)
   109→	}
   110→	return addr, nil
   111→}
   112→
   113→// RegisterInstance 将当前实例注册到 Nacos。
   114→func (r *Registry) RegisterInstance(ctx context.Context, serviceName, ip string, port int) error {
   115→	if r.nacos == nil {
   116→		return nil
   117→	}
   118→	err := r.nacos.Register(ctx, serviceName, ip, port, map[string]string{
   119→		"version": "1.0.0",
   120→		"env":     r.cfg.Env,
   121→	})
   122→	if err != nil {
   123→		registry.LogRegisterError(serviceName, err)
   124→		return err
   125→	}
   126→	shoplogger.L().Info("实例已注册到 Nacos",
   127→		zap.String("服务", serviceName),
   128→		zap.String("地址", fmt.Sprintf("%s:%d", ip, port)))
   129→	return nil
   130→}
   131→
   132→// DeregisterInstance 从 Nacos 注销当前实例。
   133→func (r *Registry) DeregisterInstance(ctx context.Context, serviceName, ip string, port int) {
   134→	if r.nacos == nil {
   135→		return
   136→	}
   137→	if err := r.nacos.Deregister(ctx, serviceName, ip, port); err != nil {
   138→		shoplogger.L().Warn("注销实例失败", zap.String("服务", serviceName), zap.Error(err))
   139→	}
   140→}
   141→
   142→// Close 关闭所有连接。
   143→func (r *Registry) Close() {
   144→	r.mu.Lock()
   145→	defer r.mu.Unlock()
   146→	for name, conn := range r.conns {
   147→		_ = conn.Close()
   148→		delete(r.conns, name)
   149→	}
   150→}
   151→
   152→// identityInterceptor 把当前请求的身份信息透传给下游服务。
   153→func identityInterceptor() grpc.UnaryClientInterceptor {
   154→	return func(ctx context.Context, method string, req, reply any, cc *grpc.ClientConn, invoker grpc.UnaryInvoker, opts ...grpc.CallOption) error {
   155→		return invoker(identityContext(ctx), method, req, reply, cc, opts...)
   156→	}
   157→}
   158→
   159→func identityStreamInterceptor() grpc.StreamClientInterceptor {
   160→	return func(ctx context.Context, desc *grpc.StreamDesc, cc *grpc.ClientConn, method string, streamer grpc.Streamer, opts ...grpc.CallOption) (grpc.ClientStream, error) {
   161→		return streamer(identityContext(ctx), desc, cc, method, opts...)
   162→	}
   163→}
   164→
   165→func identityContext(ctx context.Context) context.Context {
   166→	md, ok := metadata.FromOutgoingContext(ctx)
   167→	if !ok {
   168→		md = metadata.MD{}
   169→	} else {
   170→		md = md.Copy()
   171→	}
   172→
   173→	if uid := auth.UserIDFromContext(ctx); uid > 0 {
   174→		md.Set(auth.MetadataUserID, strconv.FormatInt(uid, 10))
   175→	}
   176→	if name := auth.UsernameFromContext(ctx); name != "" {
   177→		md.Set(auth.MetadataUsername, name)
   178→	}
   179→	if subject := auth.SubjectFromContext(ctx); subject != "" {
   180→		md.Set(auth.MetadataSubject, subject)
   181→	}
   182→	if perms := auth.PermissionsFromContext(ctx); len(perms) > 0 {
   183→		md.Set(auth.MetadataPermissions, strings.Join(perms, ","))
   184→	}
   185→	if reqID := auth.RequestIDFromContext(ctx); reqID != "" {
   186→		md.Set(auth.MetadataRequestID, reqID)
   187→	}
   188→	return metadata.NewOutgoingContext(ctx, md)
   189→}
   190→
   191→// WithTimeout 为调用附加超时。
   192→func WithTimeout(ctx context.Context, d time.Duration) (context.Context, context.CancelFunc) {
   193→	return context.WithTimeout(ctx, d)
   194→}
```

```
1→package inventory
     2→
     3→import (
     4→	"context"
     5→
     6→	commonv1 "github.com/shopstack/platform/gen/go/common/v1"
     7→	inventoryv1 "github.com/shopstack/platform/gen/go/inventory/v1"
     8→)
     9→
    10→// Handler 实现 InventoryService。
    11→type Handler struct {
    12→	inventoryv1.UnimplementedInventoryServiceServer
    13→	service *Service
    14→}
    15→
    16→// NewHandler 构造处理器。
    17→func NewHandler(service *Service) *Handler {
    18→	return &Handler{service: service}
    19→}
    20→
    21→// GetStock 查询库存。
    22→func (h *Handler) GetStock(ctx context.Context, req *inventoryv1.GetStockRequest) (*inventoryv1.Inventory, error) {
    23→	return h.service.GetStock(ctx, req.GetSkuId())
    24→}
    25→
    26→// BatchGetStock 批量查询库存。
    27→func (h *Handler) BatchGetStock(ctx context.Context, req *inventoryv1.BatchGetStockRequest) (*inventoryv1.BatchGetStockResponse, error) {
    28→	list, err := h.service.BatchGetStock(ctx, req.GetSkuIds())
    29→	if err != nil {
    30→		return nil, err
    31→	}
    32→	return &inventoryv1.BatchGetStockResponse{Inventories: list}, nil
    33→}
    34→
    35→// ReserveStock 预占库存。
    36→func (h *Handler) ReserveStock(ctx context.Context, req *inventoryv1.ReserveStockRequest) (*commonv1.OperationResult, error) {
    37→	if err := h.service.ReserveStock(ctx, req.GetOrderId(), req.GetItems(), req.GetBizType()); err != nil {
    38→		return nil, err
    39→	}
    40→	return &commonv1.OperationResult{Success: true, Message: "库存已预占"}, nil
    41→}
    42→
    43→// ReleaseStock 释放库存。
    44→func (h *Handler) ReleaseStock(ctx context.Context, req *inventoryv1.ReleaseStockRequest) (*commonv1.OperationResult, error) {
    45→	if err := h.service.ReleaseStock(ctx, req.GetOrderId(), req.GetItems(), req.GetReason()); err != nil {
    46→		return nil, err
    47→	}
    48→	return &commonv1.OperationResult{Success: true, Message: "库存已释放"}, nil
    49→}
    50→
    51→// DeductStock 扣减库存。
    52→func (h *Handler) DeductStock(ctx context.Context, req *inventoryv1.DeductStockRequest) (*commonv1.OperationResult, error) {
    53→	if err := h.service.DeductStock(ctx, req.GetOrderId(), req.GetItems()); err != nil {
    54→		return nil, err
    55→	}
    56→	return &commonv1.OperationResult{Success: true, Message: "库存已扣减"}, nil
    57→}
    58→
    59→// AdjustStock 调整库存。
    60→func (h *Handler) AdjustStock(ctx context.Context, req *inventoryv1.AdjustStockRequest) (*inventoryv1.Inventory, error) {
    61→	return h.service.AdjustStock(ctx, req.GetSkuId(), req.GetQuantity(),
    62→		req.GetSafetyStock(), req.GetWarehouse(), req.GetReason())
    63→}
    64→
    65→// ListInventories 库存列表。
    66→func (h *Handler) ListInventories(ctx context.Context, req *inventoryv1.ListInventoriesRequest) (*inventoryv1.ListInventoriesResponse, error) {
    67→	return h.service.ListInventories(ctx, req.GetKeyword(), req.GetProductId(),
    68→		req.GetLowStockOnly() > 0, req.GetPage())
    69→}
    70→
    71→// ListLogs 库存流水。
    72→func (h *Handler) ListLogs(ctx context.Context, req *inventoryv1.ListInventoryLogsRequest) (*inventoryv1.ListInventoryLogsResponse, error) {
    73→	return h.service.ListLogs(ctx, req.GetSkuId(), req.GetProductId(),
    74→		req.GetBizId(), req.GetChangeType(), req.GetPage())
    75→}
    76→
    77→// StockWarning 库存预警。
    78→func (h *Handler) StockWarning(ctx context.Context, req *inventoryv1.StockWarningRequest) (*inventoryv1.StockWarningResponse, error) {
    79→	return h.service.StockWarning(ctx, req.GetThreshold())
    80→}
```

```
1→// Package main 是 ShopStack 平台的冒烟测试工具。
     2→//
     3→// 用法：
     4→//
     5→//	# 直接对 gRPC 服务做校验（需要服务已启动）
     6→//	go run ./backend/cmd/smoketest --mode=grpc
     7→//
     8→//	# 通过 API 网关做端到端校验
     9→//	go run ./backend/cmd/smoketest --mode=rest --gateway=http://127.0.0.1:8080
    10→//
    11→//	# 只跑浏览器流程（登录 → 列表 → 详情）
    12→//	go run ./backend/cmd/smoketest --mode=flow
    13→package main
    14→
    15→import (
    16→	"bytes"

    35→type result struct {
    36→	name    string
    37→	ok      bool
    38→	elapsed time.Duration
    39→	message string
    40→}
    41→
    42→var results []result
    43→
    44→func main() {
    45→	mode := flag.String("mode", "grpc", "测试模式：grpc | rest | flow")
    46→	gateway := flag.String("gateway", "http://127.0.0.1:8080", "网关地址")
    47→	flag.Parse()
    48→
    49→	switch *mode {
    50→	case "grpc":
    51→		runGRPC()
    52→	case "rest":
    53→		runREST(*gateway)
    54→	case "flow":
    55→		runFlow(*gateway)
    56→	default:
    57→		fmt.Println("未知模式：", *mode)
    58→		os.Exit(1)
    59→	}
    60→
    61→	fmt.Println()
    62→	fmt.Println("==================== 冒烟测试结果 ====================")
    63→	passed, failed := 0, 0
    64→	for _, r := range results {
    65→		status := "✅"
    66→		if !r.ok {
    67→			status = "❌"
    68→			failed++
    69→		} else {
    70→			passed++
    71→		}
    72→		fmt.Printf("%s %-28s %6dms  %s\n", status, r.name, r.elapsed.Milliseconds(), r.message)
    73→	}
    74→	fmt.Printf("========================================================\n")
    75→	fmt.Printf("通过 %d 项，失败 %d 项\n", passed, failed)
    76→	if failed > 0 {
    77→		os.Exit(1)
    78→	}
    79→}
    80→
    81→func record(name string, err error, message string) {
    82→	r := result{name: name, ok: err == nil, message: message}
    83→	results = append(results, r)
    84→	if err != nil {

   265→func firstProductTitle(resp *productv1.ListProductsResponse) string {

   271→
   272→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   273→// REST 网关模式
   274→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   275→
   276→func runREST(gateway string) {
   277→	fmt.Println(">>> REST 网关测试 (", gateway, ")")
   278→
   279→	client := &http.Client{Timeout: 15 * time.Second}
   280→
   281→	// 健康检查
   282→	var health map[string]any
   283→	getJSON(client, gateway+"/healthz", "", &health)
   284→	record("GET /healthz", nil, fmt.Sprintf("status=%v", health["status"]))
   285→
   286→	// 未登录访问受保护接口
   287→	resp, err := client.Get(gateway + "/api/v1/cart")
   288→	if err != nil {
   289→		record("未登录访问购物车应被拒绝", err, "")
   290→	} else {
   291→		body, _ := io.ReadAll(resp.Body)
   292→		_ = resp.Body.Close()
   293→		if resp.StatusCode != http.StatusUnauthorized {
   294→			record("未登录访问购物车应被拒绝",
   295→				fmt.Errorf("期望 401，实际 %d: %s", resp.StatusCode, truncate(string(body), 120)), "")
   296→		} else {
   297→			record("未登录访问购物车应被拒绝", nil, "已正确返回 401")
   298→		}
   299→	}
   300→}
   301→
   302→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   303→// 业务流模式
   304→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   305→
   306→func runFlow(gateway string) {
   307→	fmt.Println(">>> 业务流测试 (", gateway, ")")
   308→
   309→	client := &http.Client{Timeout: 15 * time.Second}
   310→
   311→	// 登录取 token
   312→	var loginResp struct {
   313→		Code    int    `json:"code"`
   314→		Message string `json:"message"`
   315→		Data    struct {
   316→			AccessToken string `json:"access_token"`
   317→			User        struct {
   318→				Id       int64  `json:"id"`
   319→				Nickname string `json:"nickname"`
   320→			} `json:"user"`
   321→		} `json:"data"`
   322→	}
   323→	postJSON(client, gateway+"/api/v1/auth/login",
   324→		map[string]string{"username": "demo", "password": "demo123456"}, &loginResp)
   325→	if loginResp.Code != 0 {
   326→		record("登录并获取令牌", fmt.Errorf("code=%d msg=%s", loginResp.Code, loginResp.Message), "")
   327→		return
   328→	}
   329→	record("登录并获取令牌", nil, fmt.Sprintf("用户=%s", loginResp.Data.User.Nickname))
   330→
   331→	token := loginResp.Data.AccessToken
   332→
   333→	// 拉取用户资料
   334→	var profile struct {
   335→		Code int `json:"code"`
   336→		Data struct {
   337→			Id       int64  `json:"id"`
   338→			Nickname string `json:"nickname"`
   339→			Points   int32  `json:"points"`
   340→		} `json:"data"`
   341→	}
   342→	getJSON(client, gateway+"/api/v1/users/me", token, &profile)
   343→	record("GET /api/v1/users/me", nil,
   344→		fmt.Sprintf("code=%d 昵称=%s 积分=%d", profile.Code, profile.Data.Nickname, profile.Data.Points))
   345→}
   346→
   347→func getJSON(client *http.Client, url, token string, out any) {
   348→	req, err := http.NewRequest(http.MethodGet, url, nil)
   349→	if err != nil {
   350→		return
   351→	}
   352→	if token != "" {
   353→		req.Header.Set("Authorization", "Bearer "+token)
   354→	}
   355→	resp, err := client.Do(req)
   356→	if err != nil {
   357→		return
   358→	}
   359→	defer func() { _ = resp.Body.Close() }()
   360→	body, _ := io.ReadAll(resp.Body)
   361→	_ = json.Unmarshal(body, out)
   362→}
   363→
   364→func postJSON(client *http.Client, url string, payload any, out any) {
   365→	data, _ := json.Marshal(payload)
   366→	req, err := http.NewRequest(http.MethodPost, url, bytes.NewReader(data))
   367→	if err != nil {
   368→		return
   369→	}
   370→	req.Header.Set("Content-Type", "application/json")
   371→	resp, err := client.Do(req)
   372→	if err != nil {
   373→		return
   374→	}
   375→	defer func() { _ = resp.Body.Close() }()
   376→	body, _ := io.ReadAll(resp.Body)
   377→	_ = json.Unmarshal(body, out)
   378→}
   379→
   380→func truncate(s string, n int) string {
   381→	if len(s) <= n {
   382→		return s
   383→	}
   384→	return s[:n] + "..."
   385→}
   386→
   387→// identityCtx 模拟 API 网关的身份透传：把登录态写进 gRPC metadata。
   388→func identityCtx(ctx context.Context, userID int64, username, subject string) context.Context {
   389→	return metadata.NewOutgoingContext(ctx, metadata.Pairs(
   390→		"x-user-id", fmt.Sprintf("%d", userID),
   391→		"x-username", username,
   392→		"x-subject", subject,
   393→	))
   394→}
```

</details>

## 🤖 MiniMax-M3.1-Flash · 2026-10-20 12:06:18

<details><summary>🧠 思考过程</summary>

````
I have a complete picture now. Let me plan the file structure:

**marketing** package (`backend/internal/marketing/`):
- model.go: Coupon, UserCoupon, Promotion models
- repo.go: data access
- service.go: business logic + proto conversion
- handler.go: gRPC handler
- events.go: MQ consumer

**logistics** package:
- model.go: Shipment, ShipmentTrack, LogisticTemplate
- repo.go
- service.go
- handler.go
- events.go

**notification** package:
- model.go: Notification, SmsRecord, AuditLog
- repo.go
- service.go
- handler.go
- events.go

**cmd** mains.

Key details:
- Coupon type enum: 1=fixed, 2=percent, 3=shipping.
- Coupon `start_time`/`end_time` in DB are DATETIME, in proto are int64. The proto says int64 start_time. Probably unix seconds. Let me check how seed data does it: `DATE_SUB(NOW(), INTERVAL 30 DAY)`. So DB stores datetime. Proto stores int64 - I need to decide: unix seconds. When saving, request gives int64 start_time; I need to convert to time.Time. I'll treat the int64 as unix seconds and convert with time.Unix(). For DB, store time.Time.

Hmm, but on list, converting DB datetime → int64 unix seconds. That's consistent.

For promo start_time/end_time, same.

- `Coupon.enabled` is bool in proto, TINYINT in DB.
- `ListCouponsRequest.enabled` is bool — but with proto3 bool you can't distinguish "not set" from false. The task says "支持 keyword/type/enabled/available 过滤". With bool, false means don't filter? Hmm. The user service uses `req.GetStatus() > 0` style. For bool enabled, I'll treat `enabled=true` → filter enabled=1; `enabled=false` → no filter. That's the only sensible reading given the proto is frozen. Actually wait — maybe `enabled=false` should filter disabled coupons? But then you could never list all. Standard approach: only filter when true. I'll document it.

Actually, hmm. Let me reconsider: `available` bool is meaningful both ways ("仅当前可领取"). `enabled` bool — when false, no filter. I'll implement: enabled==true → enabled=1 filter; false → no filter. Document in comment.

- `ListCoupons` available=true: enabled=1 AND start_time<=now AND end_time>=now AND received_quantity < total_quantity.

- `SaveCoupon` returns Coupon. ID=0 → create; else update.

- `ReceiveCoupon`: returns UserCoupon.

- `ListUserCoupons`: status filter 1/2/3; 0 = all. available_count = count of unused and non-expired.

- `ListPromotions`: status semantics. status<=0 → all. status>0 → exact match on status column. On return, compute real status from time and override.

Wait, "status=0 表示筛选'进行中'时用时间区间判断当前是否在进行中". The instruction is contradictory but resolved: "实现时：status<=0 时返回所有，status>0 时按 status 精确匹配，并在返回时根据 start_time/end_time 计算真实状态覆盖 status 字段". OK so simple.

Hmm, but then "进行中的活动列表" for PreviewCheckout → query promotions where status=1 and start<=now<=end.

- `PreviewCheckout`:
  - total_amount_cents = sum(price_cents * quantity)
  - freight: from logistic_templates. But logistic_templates is in the logistics service's table... The marketing service needs to read it. The task says "运费模板表里找 enabled 的模板". Since both services use the same MySQL database, marketing can query logistic_templates directly. But that would duplicate the model. Options: (a) define a LogisticTemplate model in marketing package for the same table — but AutoMigrate of two models on same table is fine (same table name). Risk: two packages define same table; AutoMigrate in both — fine.

  Actually the task explicitly assigns logistic_templates to logistics service ("物流用 shipments/shipment_tracks/logistic_templates") and says marketing's "运费规则：运费模板表里找 enabled 的模板". So marketing needs read access. I'll define a `LogisticTemplate` model in marketing package with TableName() "logistic_templates" but NOT include it in AutoMigrate models for marketing (to avoid double-migration conflicts... actually it's harmless, but to be safe I'll only AutoMigrate my own 3 tables in marketing, and the logistic_templates read-only model won't be in the migration list). **ReceiveCoupon** logic:
````
````
1. Find coupon by ID → not found → errs.CodeCouponNotFound
2. if !coupon.Enabled → CodeCouponUnavailable "优惠券未启用"
3. now < StartTime or now > EndTime → CodeCouponExpired
4. if coupon.TotalQuantity > 0 && ReceivedQuantity >= TotalQuantity → CodeCouponUnavailable "优惠券已被领完"
5. find user_coupon (user, coupon) → if exists → CodeConflict "您已领取过该优惠券"
6. per_user_limit check: if coupon.PerUserLimit > 0 and count >= limit → error
7. tx: insert user_coupon; update coupons set received_quantity = received_quantity + 1 where id = ? and (total_quantity = 0 or received_quantity < total_quantity) — check RowsAffected; if 0 → rollback & error "已被领完".
8. return UserCoupon with nested coupon.

Unique key violation → convert to "已领取" error. Detect MySQL duplicate error: mysql driver error 1062. Using it would require adding it to go.mod require (it's already there as indirect). Using an indirect dep directly means go.mod should list it as direct — `go mod tidy` would move it. Since it's already in go.mod as indirect, importing it works but `go mod tidy` would just remove the `// indirect` comment. Build works with -mod=mod. Hmm, but to keep go.mod untouched, I'll avoid importing it and use a string check on the error message for "Duplicate entry" / "1062". That's simple and dependency-free.

I'll do both-ish: compute at return time (status override) — and also update the DB rows to 3 in one UPDATE (cheap, keeps data consistent). I'll do the update then compute. Let me do: in repo, `ExpireUserCoupons(ctx, userID)` → UPDATE user_coupons SET status=3 WHERE user_id=? AND status=1 AND coupon end_time < now. Needs a join to coupons: `UPDATE user_coupons uc JOIN coupons c ON c.id = uc.coupon_id SET uc.status = 3 WHERE uc.user_id = ? AND uc.status = 1 AND c.end_time < ?`. GORM: `db.Exec("UPDATE user_coupons uc JOIN coupons c ON ...")`. That's MySQL-specific but fine. I'll do it in ListUserCoupons and also in the available-count query.

Simpler and DB-agnostic: compute at return time. I'll do the join-update for correctness plus return-time computation as a safety. Let me keep it simple: do the join update (single Exec) + compute in Go. Fine.
````

````
```go
package marketing

import (
	"errors"
	"strings"

	"github.com/shopstack/platform/backend/internal/errs"
)

// isDuplicateKey 判断错误是否为唯一键冲突。
func isDuplicateKey(err error) bool {
	if err == nil {
		return false
	}
	msg := err.Error()
	return strings.Contains(msg, "Duplicate entry") || strings.Contains(msg, "1062")
}

// asBizError 把错误还原为业务错误。
func asBizError(err error, target **errs.Error) bool {
	return errors.As(err, target)
}
```

Simpler: just use errors.As in service and drop asBizError. But I already reference asBizError. I'll keep it as a small helper — it's fine and readable. Actually let me simplify the service to use errors.As directly and remove the helper. Hmm, keeping asBizError is fine but adds indirection. I'll keep it simple: define both helpers in util.go.

Wait — also `errs.Error.Unwrap()` returns nil cause; `errors.As(err, &bizErr)` where err is `*errs.Error` → matches directly. Good.
````

````
The cleanest with the bootstrap constraint: a holder struct.

```go
// ServiceHolder 持有服务实例，供事件回调延迟获取。
type ServiceHolder struct {
	svc *Service
}
func (h *ServiceHolder) Set(s *Service) { h.svc = s }
func (h *ServiceHolder) Get() *Service { return h.svc }
```
And Consumers(queueName string, holder *ServiceHolder) with handler:
```go
Handler: func(ctx, event) error {
	svc := holder.Get()
	if svc == nil { return errs.New(errs.CodeUnavailable, "服务尚未就绪") }
	return handleOrderPaid(ctx, event, svc)
}
```
This avoids the global-variable pattern and is race-free enough (set before the process serves traffic; the MQ consumer might fire before Setup → we return an error → the message is requeued, which is fine).

```go
// ServiceHolder 持有服务实例，供事件回调在服务就绪后获取。
type ServiceHolder struct {
	svc *Service
}
````

```
Now the notification service. Key: SendSms with rate limiting via Redis.

Rate limit: key "shop:sms:limit:"+phone, TTL 60s. Use app.Cache (cache.Client) with SetNX. If Redis unavailable (app.Cache == nil) → skip the limit and log a warning.

The cache.Client has SetNX(ctx, key, value, ttl) (bool, error). Use it: if !ok → too frequent → error CodeTooManyReq.

But note: bootstrap only sets app.Cache if Redis is available; otherwise app.Cache is nil. Also Redis could fail at runtime. I'll handle both: if s.cache == nil → skip with warn.

Code store: thirdparty.NewCodeStore() — Save(key, code, ttl) and Verify(key, code) (bool, string). Key: "shop:sms:code:"+scene+":"+phone.

TTL 5 minutes = 300s → expire_seconds=300.

Scene validation: allow "login", "register", "reset", "bind", "change" — the spec says "scene 取 login/register/reset 等". I'll accept any non-empty scene but normalize; if empty → default "login"? Better: require non-empty, default to "login" if empty? I'll default empty scene to "login"... Hmm, the spec lists the scenes; I'll validate against a whitelist {login, register, reset, bind, unbind, pay} and reject others? That could break the frontend if it uses another scene. I'll accept a whitelist but allow any of the listed ones, defaulting to "login" when empty. Let me be permissive: normalize to lowercase, if empty → "login". And restrict to the known set to avoid typos? I'll be permissive and accept any non-empty scene, but document the common ones. Actually "必须实现真实的验证码生成与校验链路" — the scene is part of the key, so any scene works. I'll define a whitelist of common scenes and fall back to "other" for unknown ones? That changes the key. I'll just use the trimmed lowercase scene as-is, defaulting to "login" if empty.

SendSms flow:
1. phone validation: non-empty, basic length check (11 digits for CN). I'll check regex-ish: 6-20 chars of digits/+.
2. rate limit check (Redis SETNX).
3. generate code: thirdparty.RandomCode(6).
4. save to code store with TTL 300s.
5. send via providers.SMS.Send(ctx, phone, scene, code). On error → write sms_record with status=0 and error message, return error.
6. write sms_record status=1, send_count = previous count + 1.
7. return success with code and expire_seconds=300.

Note ordering: should the record be written even on failure? Yes, record the failure.

Also the rate limit key should be set only if the send succeeds? If the send fails, the user must wait 60s to retry — acceptable but slightly annoying. I'll set the limit key BEFORE sending (prevents hammering), and on failure delete the key so the user can retry immediately. Good.

VerifySmsCode(phone, scene, code) (bool, string) — uses codeStore.Verify.

Send (notification):
- user_id or user_ids; if both empty → error.
- type mapping: 1→ORDER, 2→LOGISTICS, 3→PROMOTION, 4→SYSTEM. Validate the type is in 1..4; default to SYSTEM if 0? The proto type is int32 in SendNotificationRequest. I'll map via a function and default 0 → SYSTEM (4)? Better: reject invalid types >4. Let me map 0 → system (since unspecified). I'll implement notificationType(v int32) int8 with 0→4 (system) and others as-is, rejecting >4.

Hmm, "type 映射到 proto 的 NotificationType". The request type is int32, and the response Notification.type is the enum commonv1.NotificationType. Values align (1..4). Good.

List: page, type, unread_only, unread_count.

MarkRead / MarkAllRead / GetUnreadCount.

ListSmsRecords: phone/scene filters.

ListAuditLogs: module/action/admin_id/keyword/start_time/end_time. start_time/end_time are int64 → unix seconds.

Also: should the notification service write audit logs somewhere? "ListAuditLogs" is just a query. I could add a WriteAuditLog method used by... nothing. I'll keep the repo method CreateAuditLog and expose Service.RecordAuditLog(ctx, ...) for other services to use? It's not reachable via RPC. Hmm, that's dead code. Let me not add CreateAuditLog to the service — but the repo method would be unused. I'll keep the repo method (used by the service's RecordAuditLog?). 

Let me think: the audit_logs table is written by admin operations in other services (user/product). Since I only own the notification service, I'll provide Service.RecordAuditLog as an exported helper (documented as the entry point for other services to write audit logs) — but it's unreachable via gRPC. Alternatively, I can make the notification service record an audit log when it sends an SMS (module "sms", action "send")! That's a real use: each SMS send is an auditable operation. But it would add a DB write per SMS. It's fine and gives CreateAuditLog a real caller. Hmm, but the admin_id would be 0. Acceptable.

Actually, a cleaner real use: when the SMS send fails, record an audit log with result=0 and the error message. That's genuinely useful (failure auditing) and gives a real caller. I'll do that: on SMS failure, write an audit log entry (module=sms, action=send, result=0, error_message).
```

```
// Package database 封装 GORM 数据库连接与迁移。
package database

import (
	"context"
	"fmt"
	"time"

	"go.uber.org/zap"
	"gorm.io/driver/mysql"
	"gorm.io/gorm"
	"gorm.io/gorm/logger"
	"gorm.io/gorm/schema"

	shoplogger "github.com/shopstack/platform/backend/internal/logger"
)

// Options 控制数据库连接行为。
type Options struct {
	MaxIdle     int
	MaxOpen     int
	MaxLifetime time.Duration
	LogLevel    string
	AutoMigrate bool
	Models      []any
}

// New 建立数据库连接并执行可选的自动迁移。
func New(dsn string, opts Options) (*gorm.DB, error) {
	if opts.MaxIdle <= 0 {
		opts.MaxIdle = 10
	}
	if opts.MaxOpen <= 0 {
		opts.MaxOpen = 100
	}
	if opts.MaxLifetime <= 0 {
		opts.MaxLifetime = time.Hour
	}
	level := logger.Warn
	switch opts.LogLevel {
	case "debug", "info":
		level = logger.Info
	case "error":
		level = logger.Error
	case "silent":
		level = logger.Silent
	}

	gormCfg := &gorm.Config{
		Logger: logger.Default.LogMode(level),
		NamingStrategy: schema.NamingStrategy{
			SingularTable: false,
		},
		SkipDefaultTransaction: true,
		PrepareStmt:            true,
	}

	db, err := gorm.Open(mysql.Open(dsn), gormCfg)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, fmt.Errorf("连接数据库失败: %w", err)
	}

	sqlDB, err := db.DB()
	if err != nil {
		return nil, fmt.Errorf("获取连接池失败: %w", err)
	}
	sqlDB.SetMaxIdleConns(opts.MaxIdle)
	sqlDB.SetMaxOpenConns(opts.MaxOpen)
	sqlDB.SetConnMaxLifetime(opts.MaxLifetime)

	ctx, cancel := context.WithTimeout(context.Background(), 5*time.Second)
	defer cancel()
	if err := sqlDB.PingContext(ctx); err != nil {
		return nil, fmt.Errorf("数据库连通性检查失败: %w", err)
	}

	if opts.AutoMigrate && len(opts.Models) > 0 {
		if err := db.AutoMigrate(opts.Models...); err != nil {
			return nil, fmt.Errorf("自动迁移失败: %w", err)
		}
		shoplogger.L().Info("数据库自动迁移完成", zap.Int("模型数", len(opts.Models)))
	}

	return db, nil
}

// Close 关闭连接池。
func Close(db *gorm.DB) error {
	if db == nil {
		return nil
	}
	sqlDB, err := db.DB()
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}
	return sqlDB.Close()
}

// Tx 在事务中执行 fn，异常时自动回滚。
func Tx(ctx context.Context, db *gorm.DB, fn func(tx *gorm.DB) error) error {
	return db.WithContext(ctx).Transaction(fn)
}
```

</details>

GORM has a special case: for MySQL, if the field has no precision, it compares only the type name? In GORM's migrator (mysql driver), `MigrateColumn` does:

````
```go
alterColumn := ...

   ...
}

Actually GORM's mysql migrator `MigrateColumn`:
```go
alterColumn := false
if !field.PrimaryKey {
    ...check nullability...
}
// check type
if !strings.EqualFold(m.DB.Migrator().FullDataTypeOf(field).String(), realDataType) { alterColumn = true }
```
Hmm, FullDataTypeOf would produce "datetime" while the DB says "datetime(3)" → different → ALTER. But no ALTER was emitted...
````

## 🤖 MiniMax-M3.1-Flash · 2026-10-20 12:14:39

<details><summary>🧠 思考过程</summary>

```
The problem: `read` is a MySQL reserved word! `WHERE user_id = ? AND read = ?` → syntax error. I need to quote it: `notifications`.`read`.

GORM's `Where("read = ?", false)` produces raw SQL. I need to use backticks: `Where("`read` = ?", false)` — but that's MySQL-specific quoting. GORM supports `clause.Expr` or I can use the field name mapping: `Where("read = ?", ...)` fails; use `Where("`+"`read`"+` = ?", ...)`. 

Better approach: GORM's naming strategy maps the field `Read` → column `read`. I can use `Where(map[string]any{"read": false})` — GORM will quote the column name properly? With a map, GORM builds `WHERE `read` = ?`. Hmm, does GORM quote map keys? Yes, GORM quotes map keys as column names: `clause.Eq{Column: "read", Value: false}` → the SQL is built via the statement's quoteTo, which quotes the column. Let me use map conditions to be safe.

For `Updates(map[string]any{"read": true, "read_at": &now})` — GORM's map update also quotes keys properly (it uses `clause.Assignment` with Column names → quoted). The error was only in the Where clause. The MarkRead update also uses a Where with "id = ? AND user_id = ?" (no `read`), and it worked (the error at repo.go:64 is CountUnread, repo.go:81 is MarkAllRead's Where).

So the fixes:
- CountUnread: `Where("user_id = ? AND `read` = ?", ...)`. I'll use a backtick-quoted literal. In Go: "Where(\"user_id = ? AND `read` = ?\", userID, false)". Backticks inside a double-quoted string are fine.
- MarkAllRead: same.
- ListNotifications UnreadOnly: `tx.Where("read = ?", false)` → also needs quoting.
- MarkRead update: `Updates(map[string]any{"read": true, ...})` → GORM quotes map keys? Let me check: in GORM's callbacks/update.go, for map updates it builds `clause.Assignment{Column: clause.Column{Name: k, Table: clause.CurrentTable}, Value: v}` → and the SQL builder quotes the column name. Yes, it will produce `read` = true with backticks. Actually, does GORM quote column names in assignments? In `clause.Assignment.Build`, it calls `builder.WriteQuoted(column)` → yes, quoted.

But to be safe and consistent, let me use the backtick form in the Where clauses and keep the map updates as-is (they were fine — the MarkRead test didn't run because List failed first; let me verify after the fix).

Hmm, also `read_at` is fine (not reserved).

Let me also double check other reserved words in my SQL:
- marketing: `code`, `type`, `status`, `name`, `percent`, `sort`, `enabled` — `type` is not reserved in MySQL 8 (it's a keyword but non-reserved). `status` non-reserved. `code` non-reserved. `sort` non-reserved. `read` IS reserved (READ is a reserved word in MySQL). `usage`, `rank`, `groups` are reserved. Let me check my other queries for reserved words:
  - logistics shipments: `status`, `sort` (in shipment_tracks), `company`, `address`, `remark`, `phone` — `address`? Not reserved. OK.
  - `sort` — non-reserved. 
  - notification: `read` (reserved!) in the notifications table only. `status`, `result`, `detail`, `action` (non-reserved), `module` (non-reserved), `link` (non-reserved), `content`, `title`, `code`, `scene`.
  
  Hmm, `read` also appears in `CountUnread` and `ListNotifications`. And the `Updates` map. Let me fix all three places with backticks.

Also in the `Order("id DESC")` etc. — fine.

Wait, there's another potential issue: GORM's `Order("sort ASC, occurred_at ASC, id ASC")` — `sort` is not reserved. OK.

Let me apply the fix to notification/repo.go.

Also, marketing's `preview` test used user 1001 which has no coupons; the seed user_coupons are for users 1,2,3. Let me re-run the test with user 1.

Let me fix the repo now.
```

</details>
